@@ -60,20 +60,59 @@ final class LlmTaskExtractor implements TaskExtractor {
   /// prompt's examples. GBNF fixes the order at the grammar level, and a prompt
   /// that demonstrates a different one makes the model fight the constraint
   /// token by token — slower, and worse output.
+  ///
+  /// ⚠️ `{0,19}` on the tail, not `*`. Twenty objects is the ceiling the
+  /// validator enforces anyway, and without the bound a 350M model that falls
+  /// into degenerate repetition — "Water the plants" over and over — emits
+  /// perfectly legal JSON, so grammar-constrained decoding happily produces it
+  /// until `maxTokens`. That is twenty-odd seconds of a user watching a
+  /// spinner for a reply that then gets thrown away.
+  ///
+  /// ⚠️ **One rule per line. No exceptions, however long the line gets.**
+  ///
+  /// This grammar used to wrap `task` across three lines for readability, and
+  /// that made every extraction on every device fail. llama.cpp's GBNF parser
+  /// ends a rule at the first newline — `parse_sequence` is called with
+  /// `is_nested = false`, so `parse_space` does not cross one — and then tries
+  /// to read the continuation as a new rule name, finds `"`, and gives up.
+  /// `llama_sampler_init_grammar` returns null and llamadart raises
+  /// "llama.cpp failed to initialize the requested grammar sampler".
+  ///
+  /// Nothing caught it: the grammar is a string until it reaches native code,
+  /// every test replaces this extractor with a fake, and the pipeline's own
+  /// fallback then quietly produced a one-task draft from the raw transcript —
+  /// so the app looked like it worked and merely "wasn't very smart".
+  /// `extraction_grammar_test.dart` now fails on a wrapped rule.
   static const String taskGrammar = r'''
-root  ::= "[" ws ( task ( ws "," ws task )* ws )? "]"
-task  ::= "{" ws "\"title\"" ws ":" ws string ws
-              "," ws "\"when_text\"" ws ":" ws string ws
-              "," ws "\"has_reminder\"" ws ":" ws bool ws "}"
+root ::= "[" ws ( task ( ws "," ws task ){0,19} ws )? "]"
+task ::= "{" ws "\"title\"" ws ":" ws string ws "," ws "\"when_text\"" ws ":" ws string ws "," ws "\"has_reminder\"" ws ":" ws bool ws "}"
 string ::= "\"" char* "\""
-char  ::= [^"\\\n] | "\\" ( ["\\/bfnrt] | "u" hex hex hex hex )
-hex   ::= [0-9a-fA-F]
-bool  ::= "true" | "false"
-ws    ::= [ \t\n]*
+char ::= [^"\\\n] | "\\" ( ["\\/bfnrt] | "u" hex hex hex hex )
+hex ::= [0-9a-fA-F]
+bool ::= "true" | "false"
+ws ::= [ \t\n]*
 ''';
 
-  /// Developer-facing, never rendered: a prompt is not a user-visible string and
-  /// does not belong in the ARB. The app is English-only, and so is the model.
+  /// Developer-facing, never rendered: a prompt is not a user-visible string
+  /// and does not belong in the ARB. The app is English-only, and so is the
+  /// model.
+  ///
+  /// ⚠️ The worked examples stay INSIDE this message, as plain text, and the
+  /// note is wrapped as `Note: "…"` to match them. Two other shapes were tried
+  /// against the real model on a device and both were worse:
+  ///
+  ///  * the same examples as real user/assistant turns — the model started
+  ///    putting the when-words in `title` ("Tomorrow at 3 PM");
+  ///  * no examples at all, field descriptions only, note as a bare user turn
+  ///    — the model stopped splitting and answered with one run-on title,
+  ///    "Send the build to James and Friday check App Store".
+  ///
+  /// LFM2-350M-Extract is a 350M extraction model, not an instruct model; it
+  /// follows a demonstrated pattern far better than it follows prose.
+  ///
+  /// ⚠️ Do NOT test it with a note that is one of the examples. Given exactly
+  /// "Buy milk" it answers with whichever example it likes, which looks like a
+  /// stale-context bug and is not one.
   static const String systemPrompt = '''
 You extract to-do items from a spoken note. Reply with ONLY a JSON array.
 
@@ -82,6 +121,9 @@ For each task emit exactly three keys, in this order:
   "when_text"    — the words from the note that say WHEN, copied EXACTLY, or ""
   "has_reminder" — true only if the speaker asked to be reminded or alerted
 
+Emit one object per thing to do. A note that joins two jobs with "and" or
+"then" is two objects, not one.
+
 Never convert "when_text" into a date. Never compute one. Never guess a day of
 the week. Copy the speaker's words and stop. If the note says no when, use "".
 
@@ -145,6 +187,9 @@ Note: "Buy milk"
         role: LlamaChatRole.system,
         text: systemPrompt,
       ),
+      // ⚠️ Wrapped exactly like the examples above it. A bare transcript is a
+      // different shape from the one the system message demonstrates, and this
+      // model follows the demonstrated shape.
       LlamaChatMessage.fromText(
         role: LlamaChatRole.user,
         text: 'Note: "$transcript"',
@@ -201,6 +246,13 @@ Note: "Buy milk"
         // Twenty tasks is the hard ceiling the validator enforces; this is
         // roughly that many objects' worth of tokens plus slack.
         maxTokens: 768,
+        // ⚠️ OFF. Every capture is independent, and llamadart otherwise keeps
+        // one native context for the life of the engine and resumes from
+        // whatever prefix the last prompt shared with this one — which here is
+        // the entire system message. Re-ingesting ~250 tokens costs a fraction
+        // of a second; a capture that inherits state from the one before it
+        // costs the user's trust.
+        reusePromptPrefix: false,
       ),
       // No thinking budget: this model has no reasoning trace, and enabling it
       // only adds tokens the grammar then has to reject one by one.

diff --git a/lib/features/model_setup/presentation/model_setup_providers.dart b/lib/features/model_setup/presentation/model_setup_providers.dart
index cb5df57..22241c5 100644
--- a/lib/features/model_setup/presentation/model_setup_providers.dart
+++ b/lib/features/model_setup/presentation/model_setup_providers.dart
@@ -1,3 +1,5 @@
+import 'dart:async';
+
 import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
 import 'package:tasuke_ai/core/models/model_installer.dart';
 import 'package:tasuke_ai/core/models/model_providers.dart';
@@ -17,3 +19,70 @@ final Provider<ModelState> currentExtractorModelStateProvider =
       return live.value ??
           ref.watch(modelInstallerProvider).stateOf(TasukeModels.extractor);
     });
+
+/// The extractor download, including the decision to start it.
+///
+/// Exposes the same [ModelState] as [currentExtractorModelStateProvider] so a
+/// screen only has to watch one thing, and owns the two controls the user has
+/// over the download: [pause] and [start].
+///
+/// ⚠️ `build()` has a side effect, which a provider body normally must not.
+/// It is deliberate and it is the narrowest place for it: the download has to
+/// begin the moment anything renders the app proper, from a cold start, from a
+/// resumed app, and after a cancel-then-relaunch — and there is no other
+/// lifecycle hook that covers all three. The call is pushed off the build phase
+/// with a microtask, uses an installer captured *before* the build returns (so
+/// it never touches `ref` after disposal), and is idempotent: `install()` folds
+/// a second call into the one already running.
+final NotifierProvider<ExtractorDownload, ModelState>
+extractorDownloadProvider = NotifierProvider<ExtractorDownload, ModelState>(
+  ExtractorDownload.new,
+);
+
+final class ExtractorDownload extends Notifier<ModelState> {
+  /// Set by [pause]. Without it, cancelling publishes [ModelNotInstalled],
+  /// `build()` re-runs, and the auto-start immediately restarts the download
+  /// the user just stopped — a Pause button that does nothing.
+  bool _pausedByUser = false;
+
+  /// Whether the transfer is stopped *by the user* rather than never started.
+  ///
+  /// ⚠️ [ModelNotInstalled] carries no bytes-on-disk flag, so it is the state
+  /// for both "never asked for" and "paused" — and those want different words
+  /// on the button. Reading this inside a build is safe: every flip is
+  /// accompanied by a [ModelState] change that rebuilds the watcher anyway
+  /// (`pause()` publishes ModelNotInstalled, `start()` publishes
+  /// ModelDownloading).
+  bool get isPaused => _pausedByUser;
+
+  @override
+  ModelState build() {
+    final ModelInstaller installer = ref.watch(modelInstallerProvider);
+    final bool auto = ref.watch(autoInstallModelsProvider);
+    final ModelState state = ref.watch(currentExtractorModelStateProvider);
+
+    // ⚠️ `ModelNotInstalled` only. A failed download is NOT retried
+    // automatically: the common causes are no signal and no disk space, and a
+    // loop that re-dials either one burns the battery and the data plan while
+    // showing the user the same error. `ModelFailed` offers Retry instead.
+    if (auto && !_pausedByUser && state is ModelNotInstalled) {
+      unawaited(
+        Future<void>.microtask(() => installer.install(TasukeModels.extractor)),
+      );
+    }
+    return state;
+  }
+
+  /// Starts, resumes, or retries. Safe to call at any time.
+  Future<void> start() {
+    _pausedByUser = false;
+    return ref.read(modelInstallerProvider).install(TasukeModels.extractor);
+  }
+
+  /// Stops the transfer but keeps the bytes already on disk, so resuming does
+  /// not pay for them twice.
+  Future<void> pause() {
+    _pausedByUser = true;
+    return ref.read(modelInstallerProvider).cancel(TasukeModels.extractor);
+  }
+}
diff --git a/lib/features/model_setup/presentation/model_setup_screen.dart b/lib/features/model_setup/presentation/model_setup_screen.dart

--- a/lib/features/model_setup/presentation/model_setup_screen.dart
+++ b/lib/features/model_setup/presentation/model_setup_screen.dart
@@ -4,102 +4,226 @@ import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

-import 'package:tasuke_ai/core/models/model_providers.dart';

-/// Offers the one-time language-model download.
+/// The one-time language-model download, in full.
 ///
-/// ⚠️ Skippable by design. The app is fully usable without the model — manual
-/// tasks, reminders, every screen — and only voice capture waits. A first
-/// launch that is nothing but a progress bar is both bad retention and an App
-/// Review "minimum functionality" risk.
+/// ⚠️ Skippable by design. The app is fully usable without this model — manual
+/// tasks, reminders, every screen, and voice capture falls back to a
+/// deterministic splitter — so leaving has to be a real way out rather than a
+/// button that abandons the user on a progress bar.
+///
+/// ⚠️ Leaving does not abandon the transfer. `HttpModelInstaller` is
+/// app-scoped, and `ModelProgressBar` keeps reporting it on Home.
+///
+/// Three things about the layout are load-bearing, and all three were defects:
+///
+///  1. **No `Spacer`.** The screen used one `Spacer()` and one `Spacer(flex: 2)`
+///     as its only flex children, so two thirds of the slack — about 138pt on a
+///     notched phone — collected in a single hole between the progress bar and
+///     the footer. The eye had no path from the number to the action.
+///  2. **It scrolls.** Spacers collapse to zero before they wrap, so the old
+///     Column hard-overflowed at any text scale above ~1.05 on a 320×568 frame.
+///  3. **The button always carries a word.** It used to pass `busy: true` *and*
+///     `onPressed: null` while downloading, which painted a blank pale pill
+///     with an invisible white spinner — for the entire 219 MB, which is most
+///     of the time anyone ever looks at this screen.
 class ModelSetupScreen extends ConsumerWidget {
   const ModelSetupScreen({super.key});
 
   @override
   Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
-    final ModelState state = ref.watch(currentExtractorModelStateProvider);
+    final ModelState state = ref.watch(extractorDownloadProvider);
+    final ExtractorDownload download = ref.read(
+      extractorDownloadProvider.notifier,
+    );
     const ModelSpec spec = TasukeModels.extractor;
 
+    // The screen is always pushed — from the download strip on Home and from
+    // Settings → AI model — so popping is right and `go` is the fallback for
+    // the first-run path where there is nothing beneath.
+    void leave() =>
+        context.canPop() ? context.pop() : context.go(AppRoute.home.path);
+
+    final String title = switch (state) {
+      ModelReady() => context.l10n.modelSetupReady,
+      ModelFailed() => context.l10n.modelSetupFailed,
+      _ => context.l10n.modelSetupTitle,
+    };
+
+    final String body = switch (state) {
+      final ModelFailed failed => failed.message,
+      ModelDownloading(:final int percent) => context.l10n.modelSetupProgress(
+        percent,
+      ),
+      ModelReady() => context.l10n.modelSetupReadyBody,
+      ModelNotInstalled() when download.isPaused =>
+        context.l10n.modelSetupPausedBody,
+      ModelNotInstalled() => context.l10n.modelSetupSubtitle(spec.sizeLabel),
+    };
+
+    // ⚠️ Every arm carries a label. The record is exhaustive over the sealed
+    // [ModelState] so a new state cannot silently fall through to a blank
+    // button, and `busy` is never passed — see the class comment.
+    final ({String label, VoidCallback onPressed, bool leaves}) cta =
+        switch (state) {
+          ModelDownloading() => (
+            label: context.l10n.actionPause,
+            // ⚠️ Through the notifier, not the installer. Cancelling the
+            // installer directly publishes `ModelNotInstalled`, which the
+            // automatic start reads as "never started" and restarts within the
+            // frame — a Pause button that pauses nothing.
+            onPressed: download.pause,
+            leaves: false,
+          ),
+          ModelFailed(canRetry: true) => (
+            label: context.l10n.actionRetry,
+            onPressed: download.start,
+            leaves: false,
+          ),
+          ModelFailed() => (
+            label: context.l10n.modelSetupLater,
+            onPressed: leave,
+            leaves: true,

+/// What the orb becomes once there is nothing left to wait for.

+  const _ReadyMark();
+

     );

diff --git a/packages/whisper_ggml/NOTICE.md b/packages/whisper_ggml/NOTICE.md
index 456e728..c6da9ba 100644
--- a/packages/whisper_ggml/NOTICE.md
+++ b/packages/whisper_ggml/NOTICE.md
@@ -40,6 +40,17 @@ otherwise, rather than silently handing whisper.cpp a container it cannot read.
 * `consumer-rules.pro` no longer keeps FFmpegKit classes (they are gone); it
   keeps only the JNI entry points that `dart:ffi` resolves by name, which R8
   would otherwise rename in release builds.
+* `lib/src/whisper_live.dart` hardened against a worker isolate that dies. The
+  upstream `startWhisperLiveSession` spawns the isolate with no `onError` and no
+  `onExit`, so any failure before the worker sends `'ready'` — a missing
+  `libwhisper.so` for one ABI, a `lookupFunction` that finds no symbol, a null
+  response pointer — left `await ready.future` waiting for a message that could
+  no longer arrive. Nothing upstream of it has a timeout, so Stop became a
+  permanent spinner and the recording was lost. Added: VM error/exit ports
+  funnelled into one `fail()`, a guard around the `ready.future` await, a
+  try/catch around the native load and around every worker message, and a
+  nullptr check in `parse()` (`toDartString()` on nullptr segfaults the whole
+  process rather than throwing).
 * Dev dependencies used only to regenerate freezed/json sources were dropped.
   The generated `.g.dart` / `.freezed.dart` files are committed upstream and are
   preserved here, so this package needs no build ste
--- .gitattributes
+# Generated native build output that is (for now) tracked.
+#
+# ⚠️ 211 files and ~161 MB of CMake/Ninja cache under
+# packages/whisper_ggml/android/.cxx/ were committed before .gitignore covered
+# them. Until they are untracked with `git rm -r --cached`, every diff of this
+# branch measures ~244 MB, which is large enough that review tooling reports an
+# oversize or truncated context and refuses to vouch for what it read.
+#
+# `-diff` makes git emit "Binary files differ" for them instead of their
+# contents, so a diff of this branch is the size of the change rather than the
+# size of the cache. `linguist-generated` keeps them collapsed in review UIs.
+#
+# This bounds the noise; it does not remove it. The fix is still:
+#   git rm -r --cached packages/whisper_ggml/android/.cxx
+packages/*/android/.cxx/**   -diff linguist-generated
+packages/*/android/build/**  -diff linguist-generated
+packages/*/android/.gradle/** -diff linguist-generated
+
+# Binary assets: a textual diff of a PNG or a ggml model helps nobody.
+*.png  -diff
+*.jpg  -diff
+*.webp -diff
+*.ttf  -diff
+*.bin  -diff
+*.gguf -diff
+*.aab  -diff
+*.apk  -diff

--- assets/images/guide_privacy.svg
+<svg width="512" height="512" viewBox="0 0 512 512" fill="none" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
+<!--
+  Private by Design — onboarding page 3.
+
+  Drawn to match the shield-and-padlock mark supplied alongside guide_voice.svg
+  and guide_tasks.svg, in the same language as those two: a soft radial halo,
+  a gradient subject, and no <filter> (flutter_svg drops filters silently, so
+  nothing here may depend on one to be legible).
+-->
+<defs>
+  <linearGradient id="lock" x1="200" y1="180" x2="320" y2="370" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
+    <stop stop-color="#43B7FF"/>
+    <stop offset="1" stop-color="#1268F3"/>
+  </linearGradient>
+  <linearGradient id="plate" x1="140" y1="70" x2="380" y2="446" gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
+    <stop stop-color="#F4FAFF"/>
+    <stop offset=".52" stop-color="#D8EBFF"/>
+    <stop offset="1" stop-color="#A9D2FF"/>
+  </linearGradient>
+  <radialGradient id="halo">
+    <stop stop-color="#2F8BFF" stop-opacity=".20"/>
+    <stop offset="1" stop-color="#2F8BFF" stop-opacity="0"/>
+  </radialGradient>
+</defs>
+
+<circle cx="256" cy="256" r="220" fill="url(#halo)"/>
+<circle cx="256" cy="256" r="174" fill="#2F8BFF" fill-opacity=".06"/>
+
+<!-- shield -->
+<path d="M256 62L404 120V262C404 350 340 414 256 450C172 414 108 350 108 262V120L256 62Z" fill="url(#plate)"/>
+<path d="M256 62L404 120V262C404 350 340 414 256 450C172 414 108 350 108 262V120L256 62Z" stroke="white" stroke-opacity=".9" stroke-width="10" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
+<path d="M256 106L366 149V264C366 331 318 384 256 413C194 384 146 331 146 264V149L256 106Z" stroke="white" stroke-opacity=".55" stroke-width="6" stroke-linejoin="round"/>
+
+<!-- orbit, in front of the shield and behind the padlock -->

--- integration_test/real_extraction_test.dart
+// Runs the REAL llama.cpp extractor against the REAL 229 MB model, on a device.
+//
+//   flutter test integration_test/real_extraction_test.dart -d <device-id>
+//
+// The model must already be in the app's sandbox — either because the app
+// downloaded it, or seeded by hand:
+//
+//   adb push LFM2-350M-Extract-Q4_K_M.gguf /data/local/tmp/m.gguf
+//   adb shell "run-as uz.digitalgroup.tasuke mkdir -p files/models"
+//   adb shell "run-as uz.digitalgroup.tasuke cp /data/local/tmp/m.gguf \
+//     files/models/LFM2-350M-Extract-Q4_K_M.gguf"
+//
+// ⚠️ Why this had to exist.
+//
+// The GBNF grammar in `LlmTaskExtractor` was written across three lines for
+// readability. llama.cpp ends a rule at the first newline, so it read the
+// continuation as a rule name, refused the whole grammar, and
+// `llama_sampler_init_grammar` returned null — every extraction on every
+// device threw "llama.cpp failed to initialize the requested grammar sampler".
+// The pipeline caught it and fell back to the rule-based splitter, which
+// always returns *something*, so the app did not crash and did not complain.
+// It just quietly stopped being an AI app. Nothing in 1100 host tests could
+// see it: the grammar is an opaque string until it reaches native code.
+@Tags(<String>['device'])
+library;
+
+import 'dart:io';
+
+import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
+import 'package:integration_test/integration_test.dart';
+import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
+import 'package:tasuke_ai/core/models/model_installer.dart';
+import 'package:tasuke_ai/core/time/local_date.dart';
+import 'package:tasuke_ai/core/time/local_date_time.dart';
+import 'package:tasuke_ai/core/time/local_time_of_day.dart';
+import 'package:tasuke_ai/features/extraction/data/extraction_providers.dart';
+import 'package:tasuke_ai/features/extraction/data/llm_task_extractor.dart';
+import 'package:tasuke_ai/features/extraction/domain/extracted_task.dart';
+
+void main() {
+  IntegrationTestWidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
+
+  /// An explicit model path, for a device where the app has not downloaded it.
+  ///
+  /// ⚠️ `/data/local/tmp/...` survives the uninstall that `flutter test` does
+  /// at the end of every run; the app sandbox does not, so seeding the sandbox
+  /// works exactly once and then silently skips.
+  const String overridePath = String.fromEnvironment('TASUKE_LLM_MODEL');
+
+  Future<String?> modelPath() async {
+    if (overridePath.isNotEmpty && File(overridePath).existsSync()) {
+      return overridePath;
+    }
+    final Directory support = await getApplicationSupportDirectory();
+    final File file = File(
+      '${support.path}/models/${TasukeModels.extractor.fileName}',
+    );
+    return file.existsSync() ? file.path : null;
+  }
+
+  testWidgets('the grammar is accepted and the model emits real tasks', (
+    WidgetTester tester,
+  ) async {
+    await tester.runAsync(() async {
+      final String? path = await modelPath();
+      if (path == null) {
+        // ignore: avoid_print
+        print('SKIPPED: the extractor model is not on this device');
+        return;
+      }
+
+      // Capture the raw generation on the way past, so a wrong answer can be
+      // told apart from a wrong parse without touching lib/.
+      final List<String> raw = <String>[];
+      final LlmTaskExtractor extractor = LlmTaskExtractor(
+        modelPath: () async => path,
+        parse: (String text, {required LocalDateTime now}) {
+          raw.add(text);
+          return parseExtractorOutput(text, now: now);
+        },
+      );
+      addTearDown(extractor.dispose);
+
+      expect(await extractor.isReady(), isTrue);
+
+      // A fixed instant, so the date arithmetic is checkable. 2026-09-21 is a
+      // Monday, so "Friday" is the 25th and "tomorrow" is the 22nd.
+      const LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime(
+        LocalDate(2026, 9, 21),
+        LocalTimeOfDay.hm(10, 0),
+      );
+
+      void dumpRaw(String label) {
+        for (final String text in raw) {
+          // ignore: avoid_prin
--- integration_test/real_pipeline_test.dart
+// The only test in this repo that runs the REAL native stack: the vendored
+// whisper.cpp binding against the bundled ggml model, on a real device.
+//
+//   tool/env.sh
+//   adb push test-audio.wav /data/local/tmp/tasuke/jfk.wav
+//   flutter test integration_test/real_pipeline_test.dart \
+//     --dart-define=TASUKE_AUDIO_FIXTURE=/data/local/tmp/tasuke/jfk.wav \
+//     -d <device-id>
+//
+// ⚠️ Why this exists, and why its absence was expensive.
+//
+// Every other test in the project overrides `speechRecognizerProvider` with a
+// fake, because a widget test cannot load a 60 MB ggml model or open a
+// microphone. That is correct — and it meant the native path had never been
+// executed once, on any machine, by anything. The consequence shipped:
+// `WhisperModelAsset.ensureInstalled()` had no caller at all, so the model was
+// never copied out of the APK, `availability()` answered `modelUnavailable`
+// forever, and every voice capture on every device died on "The voice model
+// isn't ready" while 1098 tests stayed green.
+//
+// ⚠️ The fixture is passed by PATH, never bundled. Declaring a test WAV under
+// `flutter.assets` would ship it inside the release APK to every user.
+@Tags(<String>['device'])
+library;
+
+import 'dart:async';
+import 'dart:io';
+import 'dart:typed_data';
+
+import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
+import 'package:integration_test/integration_test.dart';
+import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
+import 'package:tasuke_ai/core/models/whisper_model_asset.dart';
+import 'package:tasuke_ai/core/speech/speech_recognizer.dart';
+import 'package:tasuke_ai/core/speech/whisper_speech_recognizer.dart';
+
+/// Path to a 16 kHz mono PCM16 WAV of clearly-spoken English.
+const String audioFixturePath = String.fromEnvironment('TASUKE_AUDIO_FIXTURE');
+
+/// What the fixture says, lowercased and stripped of punctuation.
+///
+/// Defaults to the whisper.cpp project's own `samples/jfk.wav`, which is the
+/// canonical smoke-test clip for this engine.
+const String expectedPhrase = String.fromEnvironment(
+  'TASUKE_AUDIO_EXPECT',
+  defaultValue: 'ask not what your country can do for you',
+);
+
+/// Strips everything a transcriber may legitimately disagree about.
+String _fold(String text) => text
+    .toLowerCase()
+    .replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^a-z0-9 ]'), ' ')
+    .replaceAll(RegExp(r'\s+'), ' ')
+    .trim();
+
+/// The PCM16 payload of a RIFF/WAVE file.
+///
+/// ⚠️ The `data` chunk is located rather than assumed to start at byte 44.
+/// Plenty of encoders write a `LIST`/`INFO` chunk first, and feeding those
+/// bytes to whisper as audio produces a burst of noise and a confident
+/// transcription of nothing.
+Uint8List pcm16BodyOf(Uint8List wav) {
+  final ByteData view = ByteData.sublistView(wav);
+  if (String.fromCharCodes(wav.sublist(0, 4)) != 'RIFF' ||
+      String.fromCharCodes(wav.sublist(8, 12)) != 'WAVE') {
+    throw const FormatException('not a RIFF/WAVE file');
+  }
+
+  int offset = 12;
+  while (offset + 8 <= wav.length) {
+    final String id = String.fromCharCodes(wav.sublist(offset, offset + 4));
+    final int size = view.getUint32(offset + 4, Endian.little);
+    final int body = offset + 8;
+    if (id == 'fmt ') {
+      final int channels = view.getUint16(body + 2, Endian.little);
+      final int rate = view.getUint32(body + 4, Endian.little);
+      final int bits = view.getUint16(body + 14, Endian.little);
+      expect(
+        <int>[channels, rate, bits],
+        <int>[1, 16000, 16],
+        reason: 'whisper.cpp takes 16 kHz mono PCM16 and nothing else',
+      );
+    }
+    if (id == 'data') {
+      return Uint8List.sublistView(wav, body, body + size);
+    }
+    // Chunks are word-aligned; an odd size carries one pad byte.
+    offset = body + size + (size.isOdd ? 1 : 0);
+  }
+  throw const FormatException('no data chunk');
+}
+
+void main() {
+  IntegrationTestWidgetsFlutterBinding.ensureInitialized();
+
+  final bool haveFixture = audioFixturePath.isNotEmpty;
+
+  testWidgets('the bundled model installs, and whisper tran
--- lib/app/router/reminder_routing.dart
+import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
+import 'package:tasuke_ai/app/bootstrap/app_bootstrap.dart';
+import 'package:tasuke_ai/app/router/app_navigator.dart';
+import 'package:tasuke_ai/core/notifications/notification_providers.dart';
+import 'package:tasuke_ai/core/storage/prefs.dart';
+import 'package:tasuke_ai/features/reminders/data/local_reminder_scheduler.dart';
+
+/// Turns a tapped reminder into a task screen.
+///
+/// ⚠️ This whole path was built and then never connected. Every scheduled
+/// alarm already carried `encodeReminderPayload(taskId)`, the plugin already
+/// forwarded taps, `/task/:id` was already routed, and `BootstrapResult
+/// .launchTaskId` was already decoded — and nothing read any of it. A reminder
+/// fired, the user tapped it, and the app opened on Home with no hint which
+/// task it was about. For a reminders app that is the primary way back in.
+///
+/// ⚠️ It lives at the app root and not on the splash. The redirect does a
+/// synchronous `ref.read(appBootstrapProvider)` on the very first route
+/// resolution, so the bootstrap future can already be `AsyncData` before
+/// `SplashScreen` ever builds — a listener there would never fire — and the
+/// splash is unmounted the moment the redirect moves on.
+class ReminderRouting extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
+  const ReminderRouting({required this.child, super.key});
+
+  final Widget child;
+
+  @override
+  ConsumerState<ReminderRouting> createState() => _ReminderRoutingState();
+}
+
+class _ReminderRoutingState extends ConsumerState<ReminderRouting> {
+  /// The cold-start payload is acted on once per process.
+  ///
+  /// The splash's retry button and the reset flow both invalidate
+  /// [appBootstrapProvider], which re-delivers the same `launchTaskId`.
+  bool _launchHandled = false;
+
+  @override
+  void initState() {
+    super.initState();
+    // ⚠️ Read once here as well as listening below, and NOT with
+    // `fireImmediately` — `WidgetRef.listen` in riverpod 3 has no such
+    // parameter. It matters because the redirect does a synchronous
+    // `ref.read(appBootstrapProvider)` on the first route resolution, so the
+    // future can already be `AsyncData` by the time this widget mounts and the
+    // listener would then never fire at all.
+    _handleLaunch(ref.read(appBootstrapProvider));
+  }
+
+  void _handleLaunch(AsyncValue<BootstrapResult> boot) {
+    final String? taskId = boot.value?.launchTaskId;
+    if (taskId == null || _launchHandled) return;
+    _launchHandled = true;
+    _open(taskId);
+  }
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    // Cold start, when the bootstrap resolves after this widget mounted.
+    ref.listen<AsyncValue<BootstrapResult>>(
+      appBootstrapProvider,
+      (AsyncValue<BootstrapResult>? _, AsyncValue<BootstrapResult> next) =>
+          _handleLaunch(next),
+    );
+
+    // Warm start: the app was already running when the reminder was tapped.
+    ref.listen<AsyncValue<String>>(notificationTapsProvider, (
+      AsyncValue<String>? _,
+      AsyncValue<String> next,
+    ) {
+      final String? payload = next.value;
+      if (payload == null) return;
+      final String? taskId = decodeReminderPayload(payload);
+      if (taskId != null) _open(taskId);
+    });
+
+    return widget.child;
+  }
+
+  void _open(String taskId) {
+    // ⚠️ Post-frame, for two reasons. A provider listener can fire during a
+    // build, and `go`/`push` mark the Router dirty — "setState() called during
+    // build". And on a cold start this runs before the Router below has
+    // mounted.
+    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
+      if (!mounted) return;
+      // ⚠️ Respect the first-run gates. Without this the detail page is pushed
+      // on top of onboarding, over a user who has not finished setting up.
+      if (!ref.read(onboardingSeenProvider) ||
+          !ref.read(permissionsPrimerSeenProvider)) {
+        return;
+      }
+      ref.read(reminderOpe
--- lib/app/widgets/capture/blob_orb.dart
+import 'dart:async';
+
+import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_svg/flutter_svg.dart';
+import 'package:tasuke_ai/app/theme/tasuke_spacing.dart';
+import 'package:tasuke_ai/app/widgets/tasuke_art.dart';
+
+/// The breathing blob: [TasukeArt.blobFrameCount] vector frames cross-faded on
+/// a loop.
+///
+/// Used wherever the app is working and has nothing precise to report — the
+/// model download, the processing screen. It replaces a blurred circle, which
+/// on a real phone read as a smudge rather than as motion.
+///
+/// ⚠️ It animates forever, so a test that reaches it must never call
+/// `pumpAndSettle` — the same rule the waveform already imposes. `pumpSettled`
+/// in test/helpers pumps a fixed number of frames for exactly this reason.
+class BlobOrb extends StatefulWidget {
+  const BlobOrb({this.size = 180, super.key});
+
+  final double size;
+
+  @override
+  State<BlobOrb> createState() => _BlobOrbState();
+}
+
+class _BlobOrbState extends State<BlobOrb> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
+  late final AnimationController _controller = AnimationController(
+    vsync: this,
+    duration: TasukeDurations.blobCycle,
+  );
+
+  @override
+  void initState() {
+    super.initState();
+    unawaited(_warmCache());
+  }
+
+  /// Pulls all eight frames into flutter_svg's cache before the loop needs
+  /// them.
+  ///
+  /// Without this, the first cycle fades *into a frame that has not parsed
+  /// yet*: `SvgPicture` renders nothing until its bytes arrive, so the orb
+  /// blinks once per frame for the first two seconds of the screen that is
+  /// supposed to reassure the user that work is happening.
+  ///
+  /// Failures are swallowed on purpose. A missing frame degrades to a shorter
+  /// loop; it must never take down the model-download screen, which is the one
+  /// screen the user cannot route around.
+  Future<void> _warmCache() async {
+    for (int i = 0; i < TasukeArt.blobFrameCount; i++) {
+      try {
+        await SvgAssetLoader(TasukeArt.blobFrame(i)).loadBytes(null);
+      } on Object {
+        // Intentionally ignored — see above.
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  @override
+  void didChangeDependencies() {
+    super.didChangeDependencies();
+    // Honour "reduce motion". A looping blob is precisely the kind of ambient
+    // movement that setting exists to stop, and the screens that host it stay
+    // legible frozen on frame one.
+    if (MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)) {

+    } else if (!_controller.isAnimating) {

+                  _Frame(index: current, opacity: 1),
+                  _Frame(index: next, opacity: blend),
+                ],
+              );
+            },

+      ),
+    );
+  }

--- lib/app/widgets/tasuke_art.dart
+import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
+import 'package:flutter_svg/flutter_svg.dart';
+
+/// Every vector illustration the app ships, by name.
+///
+/// Screens name a member here rather than a string literal, for the same reason
+/// they never write `Text('Save')`: a mistyped asset path is not a compile
+/// error, it is an "Unable to load asset" exception on one screen of one build,
+/// and `asset_manifest_test.dart` can only check paths it can enumerate.
+abstract final class TasukeArt {
+  /// Onboarding page 1 — a microphone ringed by a waveform.
+  static const String guideVoice = 'assets/images/guide_voice.svg';
+
+  /// Onboarding page 2 — three task cards with ticked checks.
+  static const String guideTasks = 'assets/images/guide_tasks.svg';
+
+  /// Onboarding page 3 — a shield with a padlock.
+  static const String guidePrivacy = 'assets/images/guide_privacy.svg';
+
+  /// Frames of the breathing blob, in loop order.
+  ///
+  /// Hand-drawn frames rather than a Dart-side morph: the blob's outline, its
+  /// inner core and its specular highlight all move independently between
+  /// frames, and interpolating three of those in code produces a wobble, not a
+  /// breath.
+  ///
+  /// ⚠️ Both artboards this app has been given ship a closing frame that is
+  /// byte-identical to the opening one — the loop supplies that for free by
+  /// wrapping — so the duplicate is dropped on the way in. Animating it held
+  /// one image for an extra beat once per cycle, and that hitch is what made
+  /// the breathing read as broken rather than as slow.
+  /// `art_test.dart` fails if two frames are ever identical again.
+  static const int blobFrameCount = 8;
+
+  static String blobFrame(int index) {
+    assert(
+      index >= 0 && index < blobFrameCount,
+      'frame $index is out of range',
+    );
+    final String number = (index + 1).toString().padLeft(2, '0');
+    return 'assets/images/blob/blob_frame_$number.svg';
+  }
+
+  /// Every asset above, for the manifest test.
+  static List<String> get all => <String>[
+    guideVoice,
+    guideTasks,
+    guidePrivacy,
+    for (int i = 0; i < blobFrameCount; i++) blobFrame(i),
+  ];
+}
+
+/// One of [TasukeArt]'s pictures, sized and inert.
+///
+/// ⚠️ Decoration, so it is excluded from semantics and takes no taps. The
+/// headline underneath each illustration already says what the page is about;
+/// a screen reader that also announces "image" here reads the page twice.
+class SvgIllustration extends StatelessWidget {
+  const SvgIllustration({required this.asset, required this.size, super.key});
+
+  final String asset;
+  final double size;
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
+    return ExcludeSemantics(
+      child: IgnorePointer(
+        // ⚠️ The SizedBox, not just `SvgPicture`'s own width/height. A picture
+        // that has not parsed yet renders its placeholder and takes whatever
+        // space is going, so without this the headline below jumps upward on
+        // the first frame of every onboarding page and settles a frame later.
+        child: SizedBox.square(
+          dimension: size,
+          child: SvgPicture.asset(
+            asset,
+            fit: BoxFit.contain,
+            // The art is square and already carries its own padding; letting
+            // it grow with the text scale would push the headline off the page.
+            //
+            // ⚠️ No `colorFilter`. Every illustration is multi-colour by
+            // design, and a filter would flatten the gradients to one tint.
+          ),
+        ),
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+}

+/// The always-on strip that reports the language-model download.
+///
+/// ⚠️ It stays on screen for as long as the model is not ready — through every
+/// tab, across relaunches, and while the transfer is paused or has failed. It
+/// is not dismissible, and that is the point: until this bar goes away the mic
+/// button does nothing, and a status that the user can hide is a status they
+/// will not have when they wonder why.
+///
+/// It disappears on [ModelReady] and only then.
+class ModelProgressBar extends ConsumerWidget {
+  const ModelProgressBar({super.key});
+
+  @override
+  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
+    final ModelState state = ref.watch(extractorDownloadProvider);
+    if (state is ModelReady) return const SizedBox.shrink();
+
+    final (
+      String title,
+      String? action,
+      VoidCallback? onAction,
+    ) = switch (state) {
+      ModelDownloading(:final int percent) => (
+        context.l10n.modelSetupPreparing(percent),
+        context.l10n.actionPause,
+        () => ref.read(extractorDownloadProvider.notifier).pause(),
+      ),
+      ModelFailed(:final String message, :final bool canRetry) => (
+        message,
+        canRetry ? context.l10n.actionRetry : null,
+        canRetry
+            ? () => ref.read(extractorDownloadProvider.notifier).start()
+            : null,
+      ),
+      // Covers both "never started" and "paused by the user". The auto
+      // start means the first is usually a single frame; the second can
+      // last as long as the user likes, which is why it offers Resume.
+      ModelNotInstalled() || ModelReady() => (
+        context.l10n.modelBarPaused,
+        context.l10n.actionResume,
+        () => ref.read(extractorDownloadProvider.notifier).start(),
+      ),
+    };
+
+    // 0, not null, when the transfer is not running: an indeterminate bar
+    // sweeping forever says "working" about a download that is stopped.
+    final double? value = state is ModelDownloading ? state.progress : 0;
+
+    return Padding(
+      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
+        TasukeSpacing.gutter,
+        TasukeSpacing.sm,
+        TasukeSpacing.gutter,
+        0,
+      ),
+      child: DecoratedBox(
+        decoration: BoxDecoration(
+          color: TasukeColors.primaryTint,
+          borderRadius: TasukeRadii.rField,
